2026-09-02 Mike: "commit everything ... as a public repo with no
copyright material in the public repo." Executed on native1 at 5a5eae7;
`public` = orphan commit 657274c built from that tree with
`git commit-tree` (working tree untouched). Origin is
https://github.com/mholzinger/sega16-2-32x.git; push only when Mike
asks ([[no-unprompted-push]]).

**Why:** native1/unpair-rebase history (2.5 GB pack) holds the Genesis
ROM (megadriveref/), speech wavs, capture.mov, mamecap frames — they
can never be published. The public branch is regenerated from the
current tree each time; it has no shared history with native1.

**How to apply:** ignored + untracked as Sega-derived: megadriveref/,
sndtest sound tables + speech PCM (speech_bank.c, sndmap_data.h,
test_track.h, render_*.h, pcm_clean/), discover/palscenes + glow dumps,
sh_src/pal_scenes.h (palette image = game data), screenshots_*/,
parity*/, fpgascreens/, docs_*_proof.png, patcher reports,
harvested_handlers.txt. KEPT as ours: tile_classes.h, sprbake.h,
md_sprart.h, md_sprart_info.h, glow_tab.h (indices/classes/rules),
discover/*.csv (decode keys), inp/*.inp. `make tables` regenerates the
ROM-derived inputs; pal_scenes.h needs three PAL_SH dumps (SDRAM
0x27000, 4 KB) named normal/boss_smoke/transform. To refresh public:
`T=$(git rev-parse HEAD^{tree}); git branch -f public $(git commit-tree $T -m ...)`

Bash Read the public-branch policy section
IN
cd /Users/mikeholzinger/src/sega16-2-32x; sed -n 478,500p README.md

OUT
- Everything generated FROM the ROMs or captured from the running game
  is ignored too: the sound tables and speech PCM under `sndtest/`,
  the baked sprite, tile, palette and glow headers, the palette-scene
  dumps under `discover/`, screenshot corpora, parity captures, and
  the patcher's reports. `make tables` rebuilds them locally.
- No rom file, ROM image, capture, or audio is published. Users build
  their own from a ROM set they own.
- The public branch is `public`. It is an orphan with a clean history;
  the working branches carry captures in their history and are not
  pushed.

---

## Where things are written down

- `ARCHITECTURE.md`: how the 32X library draws a frame, where this port
  sits, and the pivot. Start here.
- `docs/design/NATIVE.md`: the whole-frame pipeline.
- `TOOLKIT.md`: the reusable kit inventory.
- `docs/sound/SOUND_DRIVER.md`, `docs/sound/SOUND.md`: the decoded Z80 driver and the sound
  plan.
- `docs/log/LOOP.md`, `docs/log/LOOP6.md` through `docs/log/LOOP26.md`: the working log, newest
  last, each with a negative-results section.

Bash Add the public refresh script with the exclusion, the README note, the build-dir card; commit
IN
cd /Users/mikeholzinger/src/sega16-2-32x; cat > tools/public_refresh.sh <<'EOF'
#!/bin/sh
# Rebuild the orphan `public` branch from the current HEAD tree minus the
# Sega-derived paths that are tracked on the working branch. The public branch has
# no shared history with the working branches (their history carries captures).
#   tools/public_refresh.sh            # rebuilds `public`; push only when asked
# EXCLUDED (tracked here, never published):
#   tests/demo-b/arcade/   the arcade's work RAM at known frames (MAME) -- game data
set -e
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
EXCLUDE="tests/demo-b/arcade"
export GIT_INDEX_FILE=$(mktemp)
git read-tree HEAD
for p in $EXCLUDE; do git rm -r -q --cached "$p" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true; done
T=$(git write-tree)
rm -f "$GIT_INDEX_FILE"; unset GIT_INDEX_FILE
C=$(git commit-tree "$T" -m "public: $(git rev-parse --short HEAD) minus Sega-derived paths")
git branch -f public "$C"
echo "public -> $C (tree $T); excluded: $EXCLUDE"
EOF
chmod +x tools/public_refresh.sh; python3 - <<'EOF'
p='/Users/mikeholzinger/src/sega16-2-32x/README.md'; s=open(p).read()
old="- The public branch is `public`. It is an orphan with a clean history;\n  the working branches carry captures in their history and are not\n  pushed.\n"
new=old+"- `tools/public_refresh.sh` rebuilds it from HEAD and drops the tracked\n  Sega-derived paths it lists (`tests/demo-b/arcade/`, the arcade work-RAM\n  dumps the demo check diffs against).\n"
assert s.count(old)==1; open(p,'w').write(s.replace(old,new))
p='/Users/mikeholzinger/src/sega16-2-32x/STATE.md'; s=open(p).read()
anchor="    OTHER-FORM ATTACKS    2026-10-04: the hero's attack lookups for the other beast"
card="""    BUILD DIR OUTPUTS     Mike, 2026-10-04: "generated files write under build/, not
                          into sh_src/ or md_src/". Today the build regenerates
                          TRACKED files in the source trees and some tools write
                          beside their own path; the shadow tree (tools/
                          shadow_tree.sh, `make port`) copies sh_src/ md_src/ tools/
                          to keep an out-of-tree build from writing through
                          symlinks. It was added for sh_src/portfull/* (cport_prep
                          and cport_layout write the unit and layout.ld there),
                          md_src/game_irq.h (the patcher) and sh_src/tiles.bin (the
                          art baker), plus tools/patch_report.txt. The shadow stays
                          until this card lands. Not built.
"""
assert s.count(anchor)==1; open(p,'w').write(s.replace(anchor,card+anchor)); print('card added')
EOF

Bash Extract every whole-function hand derivation into sh_src/portfull/hand/
IN
cd /Users/mikeholzinger/src/sega16-2-32x; mkdir -p sh_src/portfull/hand; python3 - <<'EOF'
import ast, re
src=open('tools/cport_prep.py').read()
tree=ast.parse(src)
found={}
for node in ast.walk(tree):
    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and len(node.targets)==1 and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name) and node.targets[0].id=='_new' and isinstance(node.value, ast.Constant) and isinstance(node.value.value,str):
        txt=node.value.value
        m=re.search(r'\n?[a-z][^\n]* (FUN_[0-9a-f]{8})\([^)]*\)\s*\n\s*\{', txt)
        if m: found[m.group(1)]=(node.lineno, txt)
    if isinstance(node, ast.Assign) and len(node.targets)==1 and isinstance(node.targets[0], ast.Name) and node.targets[0].id=='_AI_FUNCS' and isinstance(node.value, ast.Dict):
        for k,v in zip(node.value.keys, node.value.values):
            found[k.value]=(node.lineno, v.value)
for fn,(ln,txt) in sorted(found.items()):
    open('sh_src/portfull/hand/%s.c'%fn,'w').write(txt if txt.endswith('\n') else txt+'\n')
    print('%s  (cport_prep.py:%d, %d lines)'%(fn, ln, txt.count('\n')))
print(len(found),'hand files')
EOF
grep -l '@E008@\|%s\|%d' sh_src/portfull/hand/*.c

# ---- FUN_0000a3b0: THE HERO'S ATTACK LOOKUP RETURNS A0 WITH CARRY ----------------------
# prog68k 0xA3B0-0xA414: from D6 (buttons, bits 1-2) and D7 (bit 4) it leaves the state
# handler in A0 and carry SET (0xA140 / 0xA416 / 0xA4DC / 0xA664), or carry CLEAR for
# "no attack" (0xA410). The five callers (0xA1D8 and its siblings) do `bsr; bcc skip;
# jmp (a0)`. Ghidra returned a D0 fragment and rendered the carry as a stale `bVar5`,
# so the port never took the jump: the hero's partner object (slot 1, handler 0xA286,
# installed by 0xA1E0 `lea 0x80(fp),a5`) never appeared and the second demo's hero
# stood at x 0x120F while the arcade's walked to 0x12D0 (rd69 tick 0xA0). The handler
# also reads D5 (0xA14E `btst #1,d5`: kick vs punch); measured rd96 tick 0x34 the port
# took state 5 (0xA140, frame 0xA9F2) where the arcade took state 6 (0xA198, frame
# 0xAA4C) because the register-file D5 was 0 -- the site now publishes p_D5 too.
_a3 = [0, 0]
_blocks = re.split(r'(?=/\* ---- 0x)', src)
for _bi, _blk in enumerate(_blocks):
    if _blk.startswith('/* ---- 0x0A3B0 '):
        _new = r"""uint FUN_0000a3b0(undefined4 p_D0,undefined4 p_D6,uint p_D7)

{
...
"""
        _blk, k = re.subn(r'(?:uint|undefined4) FUN_0000a3b0\(undefined4 p_D0,undefined4 p_D6,uint p_D7\)\s*\n\{.*?\n\}\n', _new, _blk, count=1, flags
        _a3[0] += k
        _blocks[_bi] = _blk
    else:
        _blk, k = re.subn(
            r'if \(\(!bVar5\) && \(FUN_0000a3b0\(p_D0,p_D6,(p_D7(?:_00)?)\), bVar5\)\) \{\n((?:[^\n]*WARNING[^\n]*\n)*)(\s*)\(\*UNRECOVERED_JUMPTABLE(
            lambda m: ('if ((!bVar5) && (_a3b0_h = FUN_0000a3b0(p_D0,p_D6,%s)) != 0) {\n%s%sport_set_reg(5, (uint)p_D5); port_set_reg(6, (uint)p_D6); 
                       % (m.group(1), m.group(2), m.group(3), m.group(1))), _blk)
        _a3[1] += k
        _blocks[_bi] = _blk
src = ''.join(_blocks)
if _a3[1]:
    src = 'static uint _a3b0_h;   /* FUN_0000a3b0: the handler A0 carried to the jump */\n' + src
_pass_report('FUN_0000a3b0 hand-derived from 0xA3B0-0xA414', _a3[0], 1)
_pass_report('FUN_0000a3b0 callers jump through the returned handler', _a3[1], 5)
# ---- FUN_00017188 / FUN_000171fa: THE COLOUR CYCLE, HAND-DERIVED --------------------

== 1033,1047
# ---- FUN_00004178: THE HIGH-SCORE TABLE'S VALIDATE / RESTORE / INSTALL, HAND-DERIVED --
# prog68k 0x4178-0x4212: 0xFFFC00 holds the table (7 x 8 bytes) + its CRC word at +0x38
# (FUN_000041b2: poly 0x8810 over 0x1C words); 0xFFFC3A holds a backup of the same 58
# bytes. 0x41CC leaves CARRY SET when a copy is valid (CRC matches and is non-zero, or
# CRC zero with a non-zero table). Primary valid -> copy it over the backup; else backup
# valid -> copy it over the primary; else ROM 0x4732 (14 longs) -> primary, CRC stored
# at +0x38, then primary -> backup. Finally 0xFFF010 = the top score. Ghidra's version
# tests an uninitialised `in_CF`, copies 32-bit longs from 0x4732 (2 mod 4) and into
# 0xFFFC3A (2 mod 4) -- SH-2 address errors -- and stores the CRC through a stale A0.
# Measured rd64: the port's table sat two bytes shifted from the ROM's (f451).
_blocks = re.split(r'(?=/\* ---- 0x)', src)
_hs3 = [0]
for _bi, _blk in enumerate(_blocks):
    if not _blk.startswith('/* ---- 0x04178 '):
        continue

# FUN_00001306 (0x1306-0x1336): the player-record clear called from main_entry
# (0x5D2).  The 68K reads FFE018 (lives) and FFE098 into D2/D3 before the 64-long
# wipe and writes them back after it (0x1310/0x1314, 0x131E/0x1322).  Ghidra does
# not see the pointer loop aliasing the two globals, so it folds "store X = load X"
# away and the port wiped the lives byte that the attract-step table had just set
# (0x1EDA): FFE018 stayed 0 for the whole demo, the dispatcher's D5 button word
# read 0, and the hero punched where the arcade kicks.  Measured 2026-10-04 (rd98:

== 1206,1219
# (count, delta) byte pairs (0x10CCE, passed by 0x10814/0x10954 as `lea`): D0 =
# --A6[0x7e]; `move.b (a0)+,d1 ; sub.b d1,d0 ; bcs out ; addq.l #1,a0 ; bra` walks the
# pairs until the count exceeds what is left, then `move.b (a0),d1 ; ext.w` takes the
# delta after that count, negated when A6[0x2e] bit 7, added to A6[0xc].  Ghidra lost
# the post-increment (its loop re-reads `*p_A0` forever, advancing a copy) and the
# callers pass the raw 68K address, which the SH-2 dereferenced as its own 0x10CCE:
# a zero count there never exceeds D0, and the demo hung at tick 0x76 the moment the
# partner (slot 1) first ran this (rd101, 2026-10-04, coroutine stack: FUN_000103e0
# +0x5ac -> FUN_00011386).
_hs6 = [0]

    if not _blk.startswith('/* ---- 0x11386 '):
        continue
== 1251,1262
# FUN_00003706 (0x3706): `lea (8,a5),a0 ; lea 0xFFF036,a1 ; bra 3778` -- the four
# `abcd -(a1),-(a0)` of 0x3774 add the BCD long at 0xFFF032 (the kill's points, set by
# the spawner: 0x500, 0x100 ...) into the player record's pending bonus at +4..+7.
# Ghidra wrote four separate byte adds through `cRamfffff035..32` -- names cport_prep

def cut(start, end, keep_end=False):
    global s
    i=s.index(start); j=s.index(end, i)+len(end)
    s=s[:i]+(end if keep_end else '')+s[j:]

    "_pass_report('FUN_00004178 hand-derived from 0x4178-0x4212', _hs3[0], 1)\n")

assert s.count(anchor)==1
mech=r'''# ---- HAND-DERIVED FUNCTIONS ARE C FILES: sh_src/portfull/hand/FUN_<addr>.c -------------
# Mike, 2026-10-04: a hand-derived function is a C file, not a regex pass. Each file
# replaces the decompiled definition of its function in the final text with an
# #include (so no pass rewrites the hand code), and the signature line is taken from
# protos.h so the hand file and the prototype cannot disagree. The hand code sees the
# unit's own vocabulary (w68.h macros, the register file, port_e_d0/port_carry).
_hand_dir = os.path.join(outdir, 'hand')
_hand = sorted(f[:-2] for f in os.listdir(_hand_dir) if re.match(r'FUN_[0-9a-f]{8}\.c$', f)) if os.path.isdir(_hand_dir) else []
_protos = open(os.path.join(outdir, 'protos.h')).read() if os.path.exists(os.path.join(outdir, 'protos.h')) else ''
_text = '\n'.join(out)
_hand_n = 0
for _fn in _hand:
    _pm = re.search(r'^([a-z][^\n]* \*?%s\([^)]*\));$' % _fn, _protos, flags=re.M)
    _hp = os.path.join(_hand_dir, _fn + '.c')
    _hs = open(_hp).read()
    _dm = re.search(r'^[a-z][^\n]* \*?%s\([^)]*\)\s*\n\{' % _fn, _hs, flags=re.M)
    if _pm and _dm and _pm.group(1) != _hs[_dm.start():_hs.index('(', _dm.start())] + _hs[_hs.index('(', _dm.start()):_hs.index(')', _dm.start()) + 1]:
        _hs = _hs[:_dm.start()] + _pm.group(1) + _hs[_hs.index(')', _dm.start()) + 1:]
        open(_hp, 'w').write(_hs)   # the prototype wins; the file is updated in place
    _text, k = re.subn(r'^[a-z][^\n]* \*?%s\([^)]*\)\s*\n\{.*?\n\}\n' % _fn,
                       '#include "hand/%s.c"   /* hand-derived: sh_src/portfull/hand */\n' % _fn,
                       _text, count=1, flags=re.M | re.S)
    if k != 1:
        raise SystemExit('cport_prep: hand file %s.c has no definition to replace in the unit' % _fn)
    _hand_n += 1
out = [_text]
print('cport_prep: hand-derived functions included from sh_src/portfull/hand: %d' % _hand_n)
'''
s=s.replace(anchor, mech+anchor)
open(p,'w').write(s); print('cport_prep edited')

p='CLAUDE.md'; s=open(p).read()
add='''
## The port's workflow (Mike, 2026-10-04)

- **`make check-demo` is the only gate for the port.** It builds the port out of
  tree (`make port` -> `build/port/rom/s16.32x`, never `rom/s16.32x`) and diffs every
  live object record against the arcade's work RAM at ticks 0xA2 0xD4 0x1FE 0x228
  0x246 (`tests/demo-b/`). Report a build as its tick table plus the commit hash.
- **A hand-derived function is a C file, not a regex pass:**
  `sh_src/portfull/hand/FUN_<addr>.c`; cport_prep swaps it in for the decompiled
  body at the end of the pipeline and takes the signature from `protos.h`. Only
  mechanical rewrites (a shape across many functions) stay as passes. No new regex
  pass for a single function.
- **Batch independent fixes:** two or three fixes per build when they touch
  different objects; let check-demo tell them apart.
- **LESSONS.md gets a line only for a hardware or decompiler fact no code captures**,
  with the `file:line` that proves it. A fix that is a hand file or a pass carries its
  finding in the commit message, two lines.
- Nothing in the loop depends on the scratchpad.
'''
anchor="## Where things are written down\n"
assert s.count(anchor)==1; open(p,'w').write(s.replace(anchor, add+'\n'+anchor,1)); print('CLAUDE.md section added')
EOF

import re,glob
# the mechanism: a non-void prototype turns bare `return;` into `return 0;`
p='tools/cport_prep.py'; s=open(p).read()
old="        open(_hp, 'w').write(_hs)   # the prototype wins; the file is updated in place\n"
new=old+"    if _pm and not _pm.group(1).startswith('void ') and re.search(r'\\breturn;', _hs):\n        _hs = re.sub(r'\\breturn;', 'return 0;', _hs)   # gcc 15: return-mismatch is an error\n        open(_hp, 'w').write(_hs)\n"
assert s.count(old)==1; open(p,'w').write(s.replace(old,new))
for f in glob.glob('sh_src/portfull/hand/FUN_*.c'):
    t=open(f).read(); m=re.search(r'^([a-z][^\n]*) FUN_',t,flags=re.M)
    if m and not m.group(1).startswith('void') and re.search(r'\breturn;',t):
        open(f,'w').write(re.sub(r'\breturn;','return 0;',t)); print('fixed',f)
EOF

    if init in (0x10cd6,0xf74a,0x102cc,0xdabc,0xee80) or i<4: print('entry %2d @%05x: trig %04x x %04x y %04x w %04x init %05x'%(i,base+i*12,trig,x,y,w,init))
    if trig==0xffff: break

p='tools/cport_prep.py'; s=open(p).read()
i=s.index("# ---- MEASUREMENT: CPORT_PROBE=103e0 stamps the tick at every entry to LAB_00010700 ----")
j=s.index("    print('cport_prep: CPORT_PROBE=%s: %d goto sites stamped' % (_PROBE, _pr[0]))\n")+len("    print('cport_prep: CPORT_PROBE=%s: %d goto sites stamped' % (_PROBE, _pr[0]))\n")
new=r'''# ---- MEASUREMENT: CPORT_PROBE stamps the tick and named locals at matching lines ----
# CPORT_PROBE=<fn addr> CPORT_PROBE_AT='<line substring>' CPORT_PROBE_VARS='a,b,c': in
# that function, before every line containing the substring, store the tick (|0x10000)
# and the listed locals into port_probe_<8*site+k> (uint globals, read back from an
# ares SDRAM dump at the ELF's _port_probe_0). Without CPORT_PROBE_AT the sites are the
# `goto LAB_00010700;` entries (the first use, 2026-10-04). Never on a candidate build.
_PROBE = (os.getenv('CPORT_PROBE') or '').strip()
if _PROBE:
    _pat = os.getenv('CPORT_PROBE_AT') or 'goto LAB_00010700;'
    _vars = [v for v in (os.getenv('CPORT_PROBE_VARS') or '').split(',') if v.strip()]
    _pr = [0]
    def _probe_fn(_blk):
        if ('FUN_%s(' % _PROBE.zfill(8)) not in _blk:
            return _blk
        lines = _blk.split('\n'); o = []
        for l in lines:
            if _pat in l and not l.lstrip().startswith('/*'):
                ind = re.match(r'\s*', l).group(0)
                k = 8 * _pr[0]; _pr[0] += 1
                st = ['port_probe_%d = (uint)*(ushort *)W68PTR(0xFFF02Au) | 0x10000u;' % k]
                st += ['port_probe_%d = (uint)(%s);' % (k + 1 + n, v) for n, v in enumerate(_vars[:7])]
                o.append(ind + ' '.join(st) + '   /* CPORT_PROBE */')
            o.append(l)
        return '\n'.join(o)
    out = [_probe_fn(_c) for _c in out]
    sys.stderr.write('cport_prep: CPORT_PROBE=%s at %r: %d sites stamped\n' % (_PROBE, _pat, _pr[0]))
'''
s=s[:i]+new+s[j:]; open(p,'w').write(s)
m='Makefile'; t=open(m).read()
t=t.replace("CPORT_PROBE ?=\n","CPORT_PROBE ?=\nCPORT_PROBE_AT ?=\nCPORT_PROBE_VARS ?=\n",1)
t=t.replace("\t    CPORT_PROBE=$(CPORT_PROBE) \\\n","\t    CPORT_PROBE=$(CPORT_PROBE) CPORT_PROBE_AT='$(CPORT_PROBE_AT)' CPORT_PROBE_VARS='$(CPORT_PROBE_VARS)' \\\n",1)

== port_play_tick at 2093
PORTCODE void port_play_tick(void)
{
    volatile byte *w = (volatile byte *)WRAM_BASE;
    static uint pp_k, pp_started;     /* pp_started: the one-time setup ran */
    uint i;

    if (pp_started == 0u) {
        pp_started = 1u;
        port_mmio_zero();             /* a blank screen, not the 0xFF poison the
                                       * gate fills with: 0xFF is a tile index */
        port_reset_pseudoregs();
        dispatch_skip = -1;           /* no dispatch truncation in a play build */
#ifdef PORT_BOOT
#ifdef PORT_CORO
        /* The coroutine's own first switch-in IS the boot (coro_entry), so there is
         * nothing to call here: mainloop_one_frame creates the stack and enters. */
        /* THE DIP SWITCHES ARE NOT 0xFF.
         *
         * A cold boot read all nine I/O cells as 0xFF ("nothing pressed"), which is
         * right for the pads and WRONG for the two dip ports: FUN_0000040E latches
         * them at boot (`DAT_fffff01a = DAT_00c42003; DAT_fffff01b = DAT_00c42001;`)
         * and they steer the attract. MEASURED 2026-10-03 against a MAME capture of
         * the arcade's own I/O: 0xC42001 = 0x54 and 0xC42003 = 0x66, everything else
         * 0xFF. The port had 0xFF in both, so 0xFFF01A/1B read 0xFF where the arcade
         * holds 0x66/0x54 -- the port was booting a different machine. */
        for (i = 0; i < 9u; i++)
            *l2_io_cell[i] = (l2_io_a68[i] == 0xC42001u) ? 0x54u
                           : (l2_io_a68[i] == 0xC42003u) ? 0x66u : 0xFFu;
        port_play_dbg[0] = 0x424F4F54u;   /* 'BOOT' */
#else
        /* COLD BOOT: no capture at all. The game clears its own work RAM, loads its
         * own layers and runs its own attract. Inputs idle at 0xFF (active low),
         * which is what md_main.c:7566 writes at reset. */
        for (i = 0; i < 9u; i++) *l2_io_cell[i] = 0xFF;
        port_play_dbg[0] = 0x424F4F54u;   /* 'BOOT' */
        mainloop_boot_frame();
        pp_k = 1u;
        port_play_dbg[1]++;
        port_play_dbg[2] = pp_k;
        return;                       /* the boot WAS this frame */
#endif /* PORT_CORO */
#else
        l2m_index();                  /* parse the capture blob */
        for (i = 0; i < WRAM_BYTES; i++) w[i] = l2_before[i];
        for (i = 1u; i <= (uint)PORT_PLAY && i <= l2m_n; i++) l2m_apply(w, i);
        pp_k = (uint)PORT_PLAY;

PORTCODE uint port_frame_wait(void)
{
    if (!mainloop_armed) return 0;          /* nobody is driving; behave as the stub */
    port_watch_out[153] = (unsigned int)__builtin_return_address(0);   /* MEASUREMENT: who waited */
    port_watch_hook(0x397eu);
#ifdef PORT_FFWATCH
    { byte _a = ((volatile byte *)WRAM_BASE)[1], _b = ((volatile byte *)WRAM_BASE)[0x1822];
      byte _pa = (byte)(port_watch_out[176] >> 8), _pb = (byte)port_watch_out[176];
      if (_a == 0xFFu && _pa != 0xFFu) { port_watch_out[164]++; port_watch_out[165] = ((uint)((volatile byte *)WRAM_BASE)[0x3020] << 8) | ((volatile byte *)WRAM_BASE)[0x3021]; }
      if (_b == 0xFFu && _pb != 0xFFu) { port_watch_out[172]++; port_watch_out[173] = ((uint)((volatile byte *)WRAM_BASE)[0x3020] << 8) | ((volatile byte *)WRAM_BASE)[0x3021]; }
      port_watch_out[176] = ((uint)_a << 8) | _b; }
#endif

    /* ---- CLEAR THE FRAME LATCH, WHICH THE REAL FUNCTION'S FIRST INSTRUCTION DOES.
     *
     *     397e: 4238 f01c   clr.b 0xfffff01c      ; clear
     *     3982: 4a38 f01c   tst.b 0xfffff01c
     *     3986: 67fa        beq.s 0x3982          ; spin until IRQ4 sets it
     *     3988: 51c8 fff4   dbf   %d0,0x397e      ; and again, D0+1 frames
     *     398c: 4e75        rts
     *
     * (roms/altbeast/prog68k.bin at 0x397E -- wait_frame_flag, the one function the
     * port replaces with this stub.) The clear and the spin are ONE handshake: the
     * main loop clears the byte, the vblank handler sets it, and the handler's
     * register publish is gated on finding it clear --
     *
     *     if (DAT_fffff01e == 0) { if (DAT_fffff01c == 0) { DAT_fffff01c = 1;
     *         ... *_DAT_fffff0ec = _DAT_fffff0f4;  ... } }
     *
     * Stubbing the wait kept the yield and dropped the clear, so the latch stuck at
     * 1 after the first frame and the publish never ran again.
     *
     * WHAT IT COST: the S16 tilemap PAGE ASSIGNMENT registers (0x410E80/82) are
     * published by that block. Stuck at zero, decode_pages hands the compose "page 0
== mainloop_one_frame
PORTCODE static void mainloop_one_frame(void)
{
    port_watch_hook(0x3333u);   /* MEASUREMENT: driver frame entry */
    mainloop_waits = 0;
    mainloop_armed = 1;
    /* THE 68K RE-LOADS ITS STACK POINTER ONCE PER FRAME and Ghidra dropped the
     * instruction: 0x62A is `lea 0xffffff00,%sp ; movew #8960,%sr`, inside this very
     * loop. The decompiled code spills through that pointer -- Ghidra calls it
     * register0x0000003c, which is 68K register space 0x3C, i.e. A7 -- including
     * `jsr` return addresses, which land in work RAM at 0xFFFFFEFC..0xFFFFFF00 and
     * are therefore part of the 16 KB diff. Defined by the generated defs.c. */
    port_reset_sp();
    port_play_dbg[3]++;        /* irq4_handler entries, counted by the DRIVER:
                                * CPORT_COUNT refuses irq4_handler (it does not
                                * recognise its 6-parameter signature), and whether
                                * the publish inside it runs is the open question. */
    /* THE ARCADE'S IRQ4 FIRES WHILE THE 68K SITS IN THE WAIT WE ARE RESUMING FROM.
     * The four-call body ran irq4_handler first for the same reason, and that
     * ordering is what the banked -43 was measured with. */
    irq4_handler();
    port_watch_hook(0x3334u);   /* MEASUREMENT: after the 68K vint handler */
#ifdef PORT_CORO
    if (!coro_live) {
        volatile uint *p = (volatile uint *)PORT_CORO_BASE;
        uint n = (uint)(PORT_CORO_TOP - PORT_CORO_BASE) / 4u, i;
        for (i = 0; i < n; i++) p[i] = CORO_PAINT;
        coro_game_sp = port_ctx_make((void *)PORT_CORO_TOP, coro_entry);
        coro_live = 1;
    }
    port_watch_hook(0x3335u);   /* MEASUREMENT: before switching into the game */
    port_ctx_switch(&coro_drv_sp, coro_game_sp);
    port_watch_hook(0x3336u);   /* MEASUREMENT: the game yielded back */
#else
    if (__builtin_setjmp(mainloop_exit) == 0)
        frame_rotated();
#endif
    mainloop_armed = 0;
}
#ifdef PORT_BOOT
/* ---- THE COLD BOOT. One frame, entered at the 68K RESET VECTOR.
I'll check where in the master's frame loop the tick kicks off, since it can start late relative to vblank where the ares dump lands—I want to see if a measurement build can trigger it right after vblank instead.

     * the middle of a tick (the SH-2 starts it whenever the master's loop reaches
     * pp_due, and catches up two per frame when behind), so a frame-end dump may show

def marker_offset(rom):
    \"\"\"SDRAM offset of port_tick_done (0xFFF02A of the last COMPLETE tick), from the

uint FUN_0000dbb8(short *p_A4,undefined4 p_D3,undefined4 p_D6,undefined4 p_D7)
{
  /* 0xDBB8: one death part per call, into the first free record walking DOWN from
     0xFFDE80 (14 records); the 12-byte part record is consumed through A4, which
     the caller continues from; the palette and sprite calls run on A6 = the part;
     carry = installed.  Returns the advanced A4. */
  byte *_a6 = (byte *)_r_unaff_A6;
  byte *_s = (byte *)W68PTR(0xFFDE80u);
  byte *_rec = (byte *)p_A4;
  short _i, _k;
  for (_i = 0; _i < 14; _i++, _s -= 0x80) {
    byte _spr, _b;
    short _v;
    if (*(short *)_s < 0) continue;
    _spr = _s[8];
    for (_k = 0; _k < 0x80; _k++) _s[_k] = 0;
    _s[8] = _spr;
    *(short *)_s = (short)0x8000;
    ST32((_s + 2), 0xdc70u);
    *(short *)(_s + 6) = *(short *)_rec; _rec += 2;
    _s[9] = 3;
    _s[0xb] = *_rec++;
    _b = *_rec++;
    _s[0x4e] = _b; _s[0x50] = _b; _s[0x51] = 0;
    ST32((_s + 0x24), (uint)((char *)_rec - (char *)ROM_BASE));   /* movel a4,($24,a5): a 68K ROM address */
    _v = *(short *)(_a6 + 0xc);
    *(short *)(_s + 0x40) = _v;
    _v = (short)(_v + *(short *)_rec); _rec += 2;
    *(short *)(_s + 0xc) = _v;
    _v = *(short *)(_a6 + 0x10);
    *(short *)(_s + 0x44) = _v;
    _v = (short)(_v + *(short *)_rec); _rec += 2;
    *(short *)(_s + 0x10) = _v;
    *(short *)(_s + 0x1c) = 0x100;
    *(short *)(_s + 0x1e) = 0x2000;
    *(short *)(_s + 0x14) = *(short *)_rec; _rec += 2;
    *(short *)(_s + 0x1a) = *(short *)_rec; _rec += 2;
    *(short *)(_s + 0x52) = (short)((ushort)_s[0x4e] << 4);
    _s[0x2f] = 2;
    _r_unaff_A6 = _s;
    request_palette_update();
    sprite_build_and_cull(p_D3,p_D6,p_D7);
    _r_unaff_A6 = _a6;
    port_carry = 1;
    return (uint)_rec;
  }
  port_carry = 0;
  return (uint)_rec;
}

    print('==', tag)
    for s in slots:

-- ARCADE WORK-RAM DUMP at one frame, no debugger:  WD_FRAME=500 WD_ADDR=0xFFEC80 WD_LEN=0x100 WD_OUT=/tmp/x.bin
local FR = tonumber(os.getenv('WD_FRAME') or '500'); local A0 = tonumber(os.getenv('WD_ADDR') or '0xFFEC80')
local LEN = tonumber(os.getenv('WD_LEN') or '256'); local OUT = os.getenv('WD_OUT') or '/tmp/wram_dump.bin'
local frames = 0
emu.register_frame_done(function()
    frames = frames + 1
    if frames == FR then
        local sp = manager.machine.devices[':maincpu'].spaces['program']
        local f = io.open(OUT, 'wb')
        for i = 0, LEN - 1 do f:write(string.char(sp:read_u8(A0 + i))) end
        f:close(); print('WRAM-DUMP DONE'); manager.machine:exit()
    end
end)

Bash Trace the flame slot per tick against the arcade from its birth
IN
cd /Users/mikeholzinger/src/sega16-2-32x; S=/private/tmp/claude-501/-Users-mikeholzinger-src-sega16-2-32x/c5f81f8e-7de6-48d9-b1fa-a2f1a0e38c12/scratchpad; cp build/port/rom/s16.32x $S/flame1.32x; cp build/port/rom/s16.elf $S/flame1.elf; ls -la $S/flame1.32x; cat > $S/pt.py <<'EOF'
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""pt.py ROM slot[,slot..] tick[,tick..]  -- per-tick field diff of port vs arcade for the slots.
Port frame guess = tick + OFF (default 1487); walks +-3 frames to hit the tick."""
import os, sys, subprocess
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
ARES='/Users/mikeholzinger/src/ares-debug/build_macos/headless-ui/Release/ares-headless'
S=os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)); ARC='/Users/mikeholzinger/src/sega16-2-32x/tests/demo-b/arcade'
rom=sys.argv[1]; slots=[int(x) for x in sys.argv[2].split(',')]; ticks=[int(x,16) for x in sys.argv[3].split(',')]
OFF=int(os.environ.get('OFF','1487')); tag=os.path.basename(rom)[:-4]
arc={}
for n in os.listdir(ARC):
    if n.startswith('aw_'):
        b=open(ARC+'/'+n,'rb').read(); arc[int.from_bytes(b[0x302a:0x302c],'big')]=b
def dump(fr):
    out='%s/pd_%s_%d.bin'%(S,tag,fr)
    if not os.path.exists(out):
        subprocess.run([ARES,rom,'--frames',str(fr),'--dump','sdram:0x2cc00:0x4000:'+out],stdout=subprocess.DEVNULL,stderr=subprocess.DEVNULL)
    return open(out,'rb').read()
frames=sorted(set(t+OFF+d for t in ticks for d in (-1,0,1)))
with ThreadPoolExecutor(8) as ex: list(ex.map(dump,frames))
def find(t):
    for d in (0,-1,1,-2,2,-3,3):
        fr=t+OFF+d
        b=dump(fr)
        if int.from_bytes(b[0x302a:0x302c],'big')==t: return fr,b
    return None,None
for t in ticks:
    fr,p=find(t); a=arc.get(t)
    if p is None or a is None: print('%04x: port frame %s arcade %s'%(t,fr,'yes' if a else 'no')); continue
    for s in slots:
        o=s*0x80
        d=[(i,a[o+i],p[o+i]) for i in range(0x80) if a[o+i]!=p[o+i]]
        print('%04x f%d slot %d live %d/%d h %s x %s/%s y %s/%s dx %s/%s st %02x/%02x +24 %s/%s | %s'%(t,fr,s,a[o]>>7,p[o]>>7,a[o+2:o+6].hex(),a[o+0xc:o+0xe].hex(),p[o+0xc:o+0xe].hex(),a[o+0x10:o+0x12].hex(),p[o+0x10:o+0x12].hex(),a[o+0x14:o+0x16].hex(),p[o+0x14:o+0x16].hex(),a[o+0x23],p[o+0x23],a[o+0x24:o+0x28].hex(),p[o+0x24:o+0x28].hex(),' '.join('+%02x:%02x/%02x'%x for x in d) if d else 'SAME'))
EOF
python3 $S/pt.py $S/flame1.32x 18 154,15a,15b,15c,15d,15e,15f,160,161,162,163,164,165,16a,16f,174,179,17e,19a 2>&1 | cut -c1-400

#define LD32S(p) (((const _i32un *)(void *)(p))->v)
#define ST32(p, x) (((_u32un *)(void *)(p))->v = (uint)(x))
/* CPORT_HI: a 68000 `move.w #k,0xFFF132` sets a long's HIGH half and leaves
 * its low half. A C assignment to the 4-byte cell replaces both, which zeroes
 * the fixed-point fraction. Census from the program bytes: 5 movew writes at
 * 0xFFF132 and ONE movel, and that one is the advance inside FUN_000039C8. */
#define SETHI16(c, v) ((c) = (((c) & 0xFFFFu) | ((unsigned)(v) << 16)))
/* the 68K -> SH-2 indirect call: every `(**(code **)(X))()` in the
 * decompiled program was rewritten to go through this, because the
 * pointer it reads is a 68K program address and calling it directly is a
 * wild jump. Defined by the driver (sh_src/port_l2.c), which owns the
 * address table and the miss counter. */
/* THE REGISTER FILE. D0..D7 = [0..7], A0..A6 = [8..14] -- the same order
 * the dispatch thunks index. It exists because the 68000 threads values
 * between routines in registers and a by-value C parameter cannot carry a
 * modification back out (FULL-PORT.md 30). Defined by sh_src/port_l2.c. */
extern unsigned int _port_reg[15];
extern unsigned int _port_reg_n;   /* write-backs EXECUTED */
uint port_call68_at(unsigned int site, unsigned int a68);
/* set a LIVE 68K register before an indirect call: the dispatch thunks read
 * the driver's port_cur_regs, not _port_reg. See sh_src/port_l2.c. */
void port_set_reg(unsigned int ix, unsigned int v);

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Generate the object dispatch table: 68K handler address -> SH-2 function.
    python3 tools/cport_dispatch.py <outdir>
WHY THE PORT CANNOT USE GHIDRA'S object_dispatcher AS DECOMPILED. It reads:
    pcVar1 = &DAT_ffffc000;
    do { if (*pcVar1 < '\\0') { ... (**(code **)(pcVar1 + 2))(); }
         pcVar1 = pcVar1 + 0x80; } while (DAT_fffff109 < 0x40);
Two things in that line do not survive the port, and both are silent:
1. THE POINTER IS A 68K ADDRESS. `*(code **)(pcVar1 + 2)` is a value like
   0x0EE80 sitting in work RAM -- an address in the 68K program, not an SH-2
   function. Calling through it jumps into the middle of the SH-2's own address
   space. It has to be looked up.
2. THE CALL PASSES NO ARGUMENT, BUT THE 68K PASSED A6. object_dispatcher holds
   the slot base in A6 and every handler reaches its object through it
   (docs/log/LOOP-DECOMPILE.md 28; 7208 references to `unaff_A6` in the port).
   Ghidra models that as ONE GLOBAL, `_r_unaff_A6`, so without help every
   handler in a sweep would see whichever slot was set last -- or nothing.
So the dispatcher is supplied by hand (sh_src/port_l2.c) and it does what the
68K did: set A6 to the slot base, then call the handler this table names.
The table's addresses come from docs/proof/object_handlers_union.txt (every
handler any instrument has observed) and its names from
docs/audit/func_ranges.txt, which carries each function's ENTRY POINT -- not
min(body), which differs for 10 of 788 functions.
A handler address seen at run time that is NOT in this table is COUNTED rather
than called, because calling it would be a wild jump. That count is also the
test of whether the census is complete: it should be zero.
"""
import os, re, sys
outdir = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else 'sh_src/portfull'
entry = {}
for line in open('docs/audit/func_ranges.txt'):
    if line.startswith('#'):
        continue
    f = line.split()
    if len(f) == 4:
        entry.setdefault(int(f[2], 16), f[3])
want = sorted({int(m, 16) for m in
               re.findall(r'0x([0-9A-Fa-f]{3,6})',
                          open('docs/proof/object_handlers_union.txt').read())})

# observed in a round this build's root set does not reach

# 549 functions gained register parameters, so the table silently fell from 425
# entries to 202 and every object handler dispatch became an "unknown handler" --
# the frame then scored BETTER (34 of 16,384) purely because the game logic had
# stopped running.

{

    extern uint FUN_0000d65c(int p_A4, unsigned int p_D3);
    extern byte DAT_fffff028, DAT_fffff029;   /* placed by layout.ld */

    /* ---- player 1, 0xFFFFC000 (0xD5A8..0xD5BE) */

    if (DAT_fffff028 == 1u) {
        XCHG_A6(); FUN_0000d608((int)a4); XCHG_A6();
        FUN_0000d65c((int)a4, _port_reg[3]);
        FUN_0000d608((int)a4);
    }
    /* ---- player 2, 0xFFFFC400 (0xD5C0..0xD5D6) */
    a4 = base + (0xFFC400u - 0xFFC000u);
    if (DAT_fffff029 == 1u) {
        XCHG_A6(); FUN_0000d608((int)a4); XCHG_A6();
        FUN_0000d65c((int)a4, _port_reg[3]);
        FUN_0000d608((int)a4);
    }
    /* ---- 0xFFFFC080 (0xD5D8..0xD5EE): the d65c call is skipped when D3.b is set */
    a4 = base + (0xFFC080u - 0xFFC000u);
    if (*(short *)a4 != 0) {
        XCHG_A6(); FUN_0000d608((int)a4); XCHG_A6();
        if ((_port_reg[3] & 0xFFu) == 0u)
            FUN_0000d65c((int)a4, _port_reg[3]);
        FUN_0000d608((int)a4);
    }
    /* ---- 0xFFFFC480 (0xD5F0..0xD606). The last d608 is the FALLTHROUGH into
     * 0xD608, which is that function's own entry -- a tail call, not a branch. */
    a4 = base + (0xFFC480u - 0xFFC000u);
